review_id: 81
entity_slug: the-gulf

rating: 5
review_text: I was curious about this place solely based on the reviews & photos describing The Gulf’s construction was made up of former shipping containers!! Coming from Hawaii 🌺, we receive our goods in shipping containers so of course, I was curious and had to visit. The food was unbelievably tasty & full of flavor especially the daily special (Shrimp & Grits, lots of shrimp with the right amount of spice sized in an amazing sauce), the Fried Grouper Sandwich (delicious and not greasy), & the fresh guacamole dip (chunks of avocado). The big surprise for me was the Golden Cauliflower Bites…for $10, it was amazingly delicious mixed with a sweet chili sauce & I couldn’t stop eating it!!

The atmosphere was great especially on this beautiful sunny day. Seating areas included picnic tables, comfy couches on raised platforms, and bar seating. I’m sure The Gulf is the place to be during the evenings watching the sunset with your loved ones! I absolutely loved the way in which the shipping containers were constructed and used. Definitely worth a visit…your tastebuds will not be disappointed!!

--- entity_reviews [row 3 of 5] ---
id: d0bde2ac-e1e3-439d-9cbe-98428f0e1c8e
entity_slug: the-gulf
reviewer_name: Shari Kaauwai
rating: 5
body: I was curious about this place solely based on the reviews & photos describing The Gulf’s construction was made up of former shipping containers!! Coming from Hawaii 🌺, we receive our goods in shipping containers so of course, I was curious and had to visit. The food was unbelievably tasty & full of flavor especially the daily special (Shrimp & Grits, lots of shrimp with the right amount of spice sized in an amazing sauce), the Fried Grouper Sandwich (delicious and not greasy), & the fresh guacamole dip (chunks of avocado). The big surprise for me was the Golden Cauliflower Bites…for $10, it was amazingly delicious mixed with a sweet chili sauce & I couldn’t stop eating it!!

The atmosphere was great especially on this beautiful sunny day. Seating areas included picnic tables, comfy couches on raised platforms, and bar seating. I’m sure The Gulf is the place to be during the evenings watching the sunset with your loved ones! I absolutely loved the way in which the shipping containers were constructed and used. Definitely worth a visit…your tastebuds will not be disappointed!!
